    882.0  placement-generated	crates/voxel-model/src/materials/spectra.rs	the same generator, from `materials/spectra/`, checked by the same build script
    882.0  placement-is-not	a claim that a placed line is understood. It is a claim about where a line's file is named: a file no record names is a file whose history is in no record, and a file named by a record that only read it is placed by a reader, not by a change. The record ids are printed with the counts so that difference stays visible.
